<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Therapeutic Energy<br></strong><em>From SV2D Clay Burst</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">As long as this card is attached to a Pokémon, it provides&nbsp;[C]&nbsp;Energy.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">The Pokémon this card is attached to can’t be Asleep, Confused, or Paralyzed, and if it is already affected by 1 of these Special Conditions, it recovers from that Special Condition.</p>
</div>
